   10:55:10.42 RACK engaged, 6 slots, streaming dry (7f3595d7d6 again; no release logged after = still engaged then)
   THE WINDOW IS NOT A FIXED TIMEOUT: streaming-dry lasts from borrow-engage until DESELECT or a rack SWITCH. Observed
   ~29 s in one instance, but it is UNBOUNDED - as long as the Link rack stays SELECTED/borrowed, its channel streams
   dry and edits to it are inaudible. That is the confirmed mechanism for Observation A (edit a selected Link rack ->
   no audio). Observation B (live while editing) is NOT explained by this and remains open (monitoring/solo, or B was
   after deselect). On-screen indicator of the window: the rack is greyed until it holds the lock ("orange"), and the
   §5a-R comment says the engaged state is "Link greyed, NO audio" - so the dry state SHOULD be visible as greying, but
   whether Sean's screen clearly signals "this rack is dry right now" is the UX question for the shoot, not yet answered.

## ITEM 1 CONFIRMED DEFECT (16 Sep): slot strip interactive during the engage window (lock not held). V2-only fix.
   Installed build now = DB770DF4 (2026-09-16, pre-gain fix), confirmed on disk. Sean's repro: select a Link rack ->
   "Engaging <name>...", lock NOT held, rack NOT orange, yet B/X and the < > reorder arrows are clickable; a plugin can
   be DELETED from a rack V2 does not yet own.
   ROOT: in refreshChainPanelForView the slot controls are gated on the `remote` flag and `remoteWriteLocked`:
   wetKnob.setVisible(!remote) (PluginEditor.h:~2755), prevBtn/nextBtn.setEnabled(!remote) (~2758), and
   remoteWriteLocked disables ONLY bypassBtn/removeBtn (2775) and is computed ONLY inside `if (v.remote)` (7591). During
   the engage window the view is rendered as a LOCAL/about-to-borrow rack (remote=false), so every !remote gate ENABLES
   the controls and writeLocked never fires. Neither `remote` nor `writeLocked` represents "engaging but lock-not-held".
   Opening a plugin window is a SEPARATE path (editBtn.onClick -> onEditRequest / popBtn -> openPopoutForSelected,
   PluginEditor.h:2167/2215), gated by neither.
   ACCOUNTS FOR SYMPTOM-2 OBSERVATION A: yes, plausibly - an edit in the engage window lands on state the borrow pull
   then replaces -> applied, no audio. Does NOT account for B (audible live) - B still needs the monitoring/solo factor.
   FIX: a `ready` flag (rackLockState==Held for a Link; always true for own) gates every slot control (bypass, remove,
   prev, next, wetKnob) + the editBtn/popBtn, with a visible grey, until Held. ~30-50 lines in one render path
   (refreshChainPanelForView) + a guard on onEditRequest/openPopout. Own-channel is never in this state (remote=false AND
   held-equivalent) - unaffected; prove it. VERIFICATION LIMIT: the enable state is JUCE isEnabled on buttons INTERNAL to
   ChainListPanel - not cleanly headless. Options: (a) in-host RED/GREEN, or (b) add a small test accessor (like
   testPendingLinkPreGainDb) returning the slot-control enable states so a headless leg can assert both directions.
   Recommend (b). ITEM 2 (per-slot wet knob) is largely SUBSUMED: the knob is hidden by setVisible(!remote); once the
   rack is properly held it is visible and writes to the borrowed host (per-slot wet travels to the Link via chain-cmd on
   commit). Small once ITEM 1's ready-gate is right.

## MODEL PROVIDER FACTS (16 Sep, from MAIN's code; production=7sewsns69 may differ - I cannot read the deployed bundle).
   Routing is one shared module _model-routing.js used by BOTH /api/chat and /api/chat-stream. MODELS: sonnet=
   claude-sonnet-4-6 (display "Oracle 2.7"), haiku=claude-haiku-4-5 ("Sage 1.0"), opus=claude-opus-5, gpt41=gpt-4.1,
   gpt5=gpt-5.5, sonnet5=claude-sonnet-5. PROVIDER IS MIXED: Anthropic (claude-*) + OpenAI (gpt-4.1 for classify).
   Calls per chain-build turn (chat.js, in order): (a) opener_detect = claude-haiku-4-5 (Anthropic), run PARALLEL to a
   regex (Promise.all at 1671 = one Haiku call + one regex, not two models); (b) classify = gpt-4.1 (OpenAI,
   _classifier.js:63 api.openai.com); (c) primary reply = resolveModel(tier, chain_generate) = claude-sonnet-4-6
   "Oracle 2.7" (Anthropic, chat.js:1120); (d) pass2 (_chain-pass2) + (e) dial-repair per-slot fill (chat.js only,
   autoDial) + (f) brief-wording (_brief-wording, same model as classify) as the flow warrants - each a DISTINCT
   purpose. FAN-OUT: NONE - the same turn is not sent to >1 model or >once; the only Promise.all is opener(Haiku)+regex.
   "Oracle 2.7" = the display name of claude-sonnet-4-6, set server-side in _model-routing.js:70; shown in the plugin UI
   as the model that handled the turn (haiku shows "Sage 1.0").

## 🔬 CRASH — ATTACH-PATH INVESTIGATION (16 Sep, no fix/no rebuild):
   NOT A ONE-OFF. Scan of ~/Library/Logs/DiagnosticReports found THREE crashes with the IDENTICAL signature:
     09-09 15:31 Purple Audio MC 77 (HL Thread 4) | 09-15 12:36 Purple Audio MC 77 (HL Thread 7) | 09-16 14:50 Empirical Labs Mike-E Comp (HL Thread 0)
   All: EXC_BAD_ACCESS KERN_INVALID_ADDRESS@0x0, AAE audio-render thread, AudioToolboxCore+0x2e8d5c (inside AudioUnitRender),
   through EchoJay Link AudioUnitPluginInstanceHeadless::processAudio > GraphRenderSequence > ChainHost::process > LinkProcessor::processBlock.
   Purple Audio faults at the SAME internal offsets both times (+0x1c2391 ...) = DETERMINISTIC, not a timing smear. 2 plugins = not plugin-specific.
   (Other .ips this week are a DIFFERENT class: Main-thread / EXC_GUARD / ProTools_Red host crashes — NOT this render path; do not conflate.)
   Attach-path facts (ChainHost.cpp):
     1. Plan Create does NOT instantiate — OpType::Create calls tryReattachParked(), pulling a Phase-A pre-staged instance;
        seeds setStateInformation(stateB64) AFTER graph_->prepareToPlay (state loaded, no re-initialise after). Fresh completeLoad
        creates via createPluginInstanceAsync(sr,bs) and never seeds inline.
     2. Guard: ChainHost::process try-locks graphLock_ (passes dry on contention); GraphMutation wraps each op. BUT the Create
        op's setStateInformation seed (~ChainHost.cpp:5007) is OUTSIDE any GraphMutation — unguarded vs a concurrent AudioUnitRender
        on the (bypass-supporting AUs still render when bypassed) live node. JUCE applySettings only prepares nodes NOT already in
        preparedNodes and skips ALL existing nodes when PrepareSettings unchanged (juce_AudioProcessorGraph.cpp:441-490) — a reused/
        parked node keeps its nodeID+prepare and is not re-initialised on reattach.
     3. EJPlace ×7 in 24ms = pop staged/parked + rebuild, NOT heavy init. Heavy init (createPluginInstance) was Phase-A "stage lookup"
        at 31.9-33.1s (~1-2s). First live render ~33.255; lease dropped 33.374; crash 41.0 (steady render, ~7-8s later).
     4. Teardown overlap: old lease released 33.374 > new locked 33.254 (120ms), but the borrow/park model KEEPS every parked
        instance ALIVE (§3a) — nothing is freed across the boundary. No free-across-switch.
     5. Headless AU host: no view; drives AudioUnitInitialize/Render directly; render guarded by `if (prepared)` (PLAIN bool, not atomic).
        prepared==false would bypass (clear), not crash — so at crash prepared==true (AU was initialised). Reuse leans on applySettings
        skipping re-prepare of unchanged nodes.
   HONEST READ: fault PC is inside closed Softube code, but it is deterministic, recurs across plugins, ONLY on our Link render path,
   and correlates with plan-attach seed ordering — so it is OURS to fix (init/seed order + lock coverage), NOT "the AU is at fault".
   Leading candidates (unproven): (a) setStateInformation after final prepare with no re-init; (b) that seed running unguarded vs render.
   REPRO: has recurred 3x/deterministic. Bench repro PLAUSIBLE via a headless ChainHost(Borrowed) harness: stage rack, releaseBorrowToPool,
   applyStructurePlan with a captured stateB64, then process() in a loop — plugins are installed here (borrowhost_test loads real ones).

## 🔬 CRASH follow-up (16 Sep): other-crash re-exam + repro feasibility + FIX PROPOSAL (nothing built):
   #1 OTHER CRASHES — NOT the same defect. The Main-thread plugin crashes (MC 77 09-10 12:29 "idle crash",
      Black Box 09-10 11:52, Acme 09-15 10:31) are the plugins' OWN editor UI under Pro Tools' AppKit loop
      (AppKit>HIToolbox>CoreFoundation>plugin GUI), ZERO EchoJay frames, not our render path. The ProTools_Red/
      EXC_GUARD/EXC_CRASH ones are host/XPC. Only the THREE audio-render crashes (09-09/09-15 Purple Audio, 09-16
      Empirical Labs) are ours. Note: MC 77 appears BOTH as our render defect (09-09,09-15) AND as its own UI crash
      (09-10) — distinct incidents; do not merge.
   #2 REPRO FEASIBILITY — the premise "borrowhost_test instantiates real plugins headlessly" is FALSE: it uses a mock
      BorrowProbe (synthetic AudioProcessor), sandboxed byte-identical checks; structplan_test is file-level ("no
      processor in the room"). NEITHER instantiates or renders a real AU. So a NEW harness is required, and headless
      instantiation of iLok/Softube "_VST_AU_Protect" wrappers is UNPROVEN + risky (may need GUI/host context, may pop
      licensing dialogs -> hang). The crashing preset's stateB64 is NOT captured to disk (chat-body-debug.json holds
      fingerprints, not the Link plan's state). Reported to reviewer BEFORE building anything.
   #3 FIX PROPOSAL (not written):
      (a) SEED ORDER: setStateInformation must run BEFORE the node's (final) prepareToPlay, so AudioUnitInitialize
          allocates render resources for the seeded state. Today the plan Create seeds AFTER graph_->prepareToPlay.
      (b) RACE: seed the instance WHILE IT IS STILL PARKED/DETACHED (suspended, not in slots_, not in the render
          sequence) -> the audio thread cannot reach it, so NO lock is held across the slow setStateInformation and
          NO audio is dropped. This subsumes the graphLock_ problem without a long lock or deadlock.
      (c) REUSE RE-PREPARE: a parked node reused with a NEW state MUST be re-prepared; JUCE applySettings skips
          already-prepared nodes when PrepareSettings are unchanged (nodeID stays in preparedNodes). Fix: releaseResources()
          the node at PARK time (so applySettings re-prepares it fresh on reattach, after the seed) — treat reuse like fresh.
      (d) SMALLEST CHANGE + WHERE: reorder in ChainHost.cpp (applyStructurePlan Create case + tryReattachParked/
          borrowTryReuseInto): seed-while-detached, then wire+prepare. ChainHost.cpp is SHARED, but the crash renders in
          the LINK's ChainHost -> THE FIX MUST SHIP IN THE LINK. This is the first fix in weeks that REQUIRES a Link
          rebuild+re-sign (breaks the "do not touch the Link" cadence). Flag to Sean.
   ⛔ SEAN'S RECORD: DO NOT SWITCH LINK RACKS until this is fixed — it is a rehearsal-stopper (deterministic crash on
      rack switch when the new rack hosts certain third-party AUs).

## 🔬 HEADLESS-FORK DIFF — DONE. THE AU HOST IS NOT A FORK; IT IS STOCK UPSTREAM JUCE 8.0.12 (host-callback theory ELIMINATED):
   Fetched pristine upstream JUCE 8.0.12 (raw.githubusercontent, juce-framework/JUCE tag 8.0.12) and diffed file-by-file:
     juce_AudioUnitPluginFormatImpl.h            0 changed lines vs upstream   (the whole AU host impl, 2591 lines)
     juce_AudioUnitPluginFormatHeadless.mm/.h    0 changed lines
     juce_AudioUnitPluginFormat.mm               0 changed lines
     juce_audio_processors_headless.h            0 changed lines
   ARCHITECTURE: `juce_audio_processors_headless` is an UPSTREAM JUCE 8 module (it exists in the upstream repo; the fetch
     proves it). Upstream itself split AU/VST/VST3/LV2 hosting into a shared headless base Impl.h that BOTH the full
     `juce_audio_processors` (its .mm #includes the headless Impl.h, then adds `class AudioUnitPluginInstance : public
     AudioUnitPluginInstanceHeadless` = editor subclass) AND the headless variant use. EchoJay simply instantiates the
     headless BASE (no editor). NOBODY at EchoJay removed anything — the editor split is JUCE's own design.
   HOST CALLBACKS: PRESENT + COMPLETE in the shared base. setPluginCallbacks() (called unconditionally from every
     prepareToPlay) installs kAudioUnitProperty_HostCallbacks with beatAndTempoProc/musicalTimeLocationProc/
     transportStateProc; the callback bodies are null-safe (getFromPlayHead(...).orFallback(...)). Identical to what any
     stock JUCE AU host installs. => The "fork dropped host callbacks -> AU jumps through null" hypothesis is DISPROVEN.
   ITEM 3 (what stock sets that the fork omits): NOTHING — zero diff. The only thing the headless base lacks is the EDITOR.
   REMAINING (questions, not theory): (4) Empirical Labs Mike-E Comp (Softube/SfTb) + Purple Audio MC 77 (Brainworx/Brwx)
     are both analogue-modelled COMPRESSORS with licence wrappers — different wrapper vendors, same plugin class; note only.
     (5) CONTROL to run: Sean has both on normal Pro Tools inserts — do they crash there? If not, the delta is EchoJay
     hosting a stock-JUCE AU graph NESTED inside an AAX plugin's render callback on the AAE thread, vs Pro Tools hosting the
     AU top-level. That nesting is the environmental difference to examine next. Host-code-is-a-broken-fork = eliminated.

## 🎯 CONTROL ESTABLISHED (Sean, strongest single fact): Empirical Labs Mike-E Comp AND Purple Audio MC 77 run STABLY on
   ORDINARY Pro Tools inserts on this machine. Same plugins, same Mac, stable top-level; deterministic crash ONLY nested in
   our rack. => The plugins are NOT the variable and the machine is NOT the variable. THE NESTING IS. "Maybe it's them" is dead.
   The difference the control isolates: Pro Tools prepares the AU itself top-level (correct); WE prepare it nested. What we
   do at prepare, or the thread/position we render it on, is the whole remaining surface.

## 🔎 FOUR FACTS (191254 crash, with the nesting-is-the-variable control) — three leads come back NEGATIVE:
   1. BLOCK/SR prepared vs handed = NO MISMATCH. Stock JUCE AAX wrapper (juce_audio_plugin_client_AAX.cpp:1998-2005)
      prepares the Link with maxBufferSize (prepareToPlay(sr, maxBufferSize); PT12+ max = 1024). Mid-session it re-prepares
      ONLY if bufferSize > maxBufferSize (:1661) — "should never actually happen." So AAE NEVER hands more samples than
      prepared. chainHost.prepare(sr, block=maxBufferSize) -> hosted AUs MaximumFramesPerSlice = that max; reattached AUs
      use the same stored blockSize_. Prepared >= rendered always. The block-overrun hypothesis is CLOSED (negative).
      (There IS no clamp in LinkProcessor::processBlock before chainHost.process — but stock JUCE upstream of it guarantees
      n <= prepared, so the missing clamp never bites via the AAX path.)
   2. RE-PREPARE ON CHANGE: nothing re-prepares the nested hosted AUs on a normal block-size change (<= max) — only a
      growth beyond max (which PT never does) calls prepareToPlay again; a <= max change updates only the Link's reported
      size (setRateAndBufferSizeDetails), not the nested AUs. Safe only because they were prepared at the MAX. No SR/block
      change events in the crash session (single prepare at start).
   3. THREAD/STACK = NULL CALL, NOT STACK OVERFLOW. Fault addr 0x0, vmRegionInfo "0 is not in any region" (a stack overflow
      faults at the guard page — a HIGH address near the thread stack — never 0x0). Crashing AAE thread depth 24 frames
      (9 HL render threads, other depth 9; process-deepest = Main at 30). No runaway depth. Stack-overflow ELIMINATED; the
      shape is a call through a null function pointer, consistent with the AU's own render dereferencing a null.
   4. WHAT ELSE FIRED IN THE ~14s WINDOW (19:12:40.5 -> 19:12:54): NOTHING but meter spam. No transport start, no lease
      renewal, no UI, no key pass. EJLinkKey coincidence KILLED by the 2nd data point: 14:50 pass armed 33.379 -> close
      ~41.4 ~ crash 41.0 (coincided); 19:12 last pass armed 19:12:35.08 -> close ~43.08, crash 54.0 (11s LATER), and NONE
      armed in the pre-crash window. The key pass is NOT correlated with the crash. No discrete trigger — pure steady-state
      render, ~14s in.
   NET: block-overrun, stack-overflow, and key-pass-coincidence all NEGATIVE. What remains: the AU's OWN render, invoked by
   AudioUnitRender in our NESTED context (a stock-JUCE AU graph rendered inside an AAX plugin's process callback on an AAE
   render thread), with no external event and correct block/SR. Nesting is the isolated variable (Sean's control). No theory.

   AFTER the switch. A switch with the transport STOPPED is safe — no render, and the crash is IN render. This also
   explains the previously-unaccountable 8-14s gap: it was NOT time since the switch, it was time until audio NEXT ran
   through the new rack. Log verification of the void 19:56 run: EJLinkMeter momentary mostly -72..-98 dB (at/below floor),
   only intermittent signal, and the passive-key-pass proxy (arms ONLY when playing + signal>floor) is the objective
   transport-rolling gauge to use per arm from now on. => Every future crash test MUST keep audio playing across each switch.

   teardown; third-party flakiness; block-size overrun; instance REUSE (valid flag-on run 200914, identical offsets, inside
   the AU's own render, ~15s after switch — not immediate, not a use-after-free). Do not re-investigate any of these.
   NEXT HYPOTHESIS (ours, untested): parameters set on the AU WHILE AUDIO RENDERS (the rack-switch dial racing the plugin's
   own render -> reallocation -> null member at a fixed offset). Fits: needs audio, needs a switch (the dial), fixed offset,
   inside the plugin's render, fine on a PT insert (nothing dials it there).

## 📕 PARAM-WRITE-DURING-RENDER HYPOTHESIS — DEAD for the Link path (20:09 crash, from the log):
   1. NO parameter writes on the LINK's Empirical Labs in the window 20:08:59 (switch) -> 20:09:14 (crash). ZERO
      EJParamApply/EJDial lines on EchoJay Link. Every dial line is EchoJay V2, PRE-switch (20:08:48-20:08:59), on V2's rack.
   2. The V2-side dial set Ratio, Attack, Release (shape=wrapped, 3 controls; Ratio/Attack/Release are switched/discrete on
      a Mike-E) — but written on V2, not on the Link during render.
   3. applyStructuredIfReady = message thread, outside graphLock_ — irrelevant here: it did not run on the Link in the window.
   4. Gap: last param write ANYWHERE = 20:08:48.320 (V2), 26s before the crash. Last Link-side write in window = NONE. No
      correlation.
   5. V2 dialled the SAME plugin (Ratio/Attack/Release) on its own/borrowed rack at 20:08:48 with audio rolling and did NOT
      crash -> weakens the hypothesis further.
   ADJACENT FACT (not a theory): the ONLY AU property write that provably happens on the Link during render on a switch is the
   LEASE BYPASS toggle — RACK engaged (bypassed) then "RACK released/expired - 7 slot bypass state(s) restored" @20:09:01.718;
   stock headless JUCE applies bypass via bypassParam->setValue() INSIDE processAudio, i.e. on the AUDIO thread. Gap to the
   crash: 12.3s — not tight. Stock JUCE behaviour, but the per-switch lease toggle is ours. Recorded, not promoted.
   Sean's no-build test (switch to a NEVER-dialled, hand-built, defaults rack with audio playing) still worth running: it
   isolates ALL our writes (dial + state seed) at once.

## ⛔ BYPASS-WRITE HYPOTHESIS — DEAD ON THE READ; rack-level-dry fix NOT written (the write it removes never happens):
   STOCK path (if it ran): processAudio (audio thread) -> updateBypass -> bypassParam->setValue -> AudioUnitSetProperty(
     kAudioUnitProperty_BypassEffect) + AUEventListenerNotify. Apple's header: "Read / Write, Global, UInt32 - a boolean
     that can be used to bypass the processing" - SILENT on threading; nearest contract (AUAudioUnitImplementation.h:227-229)
     is that realtime rendering threads "must not attempt to interact with the AudioUnit" beyond rendering.
   BUT OUR LEASE NEVER REACHES IT. ChainHost::rebuildGraph (:5403) bypasses a slot by ROUTING AROUND it — only
     `!s.bypassed` slots are wired (line 16-17) — it NEVER calls node->setBypassed(). So the JUCE graph's per-node
     `bypass` (graph :893, node->isBypassed()) is always false, processBlockBypassed is never called, updateBypass(false)
     never flips lastProcessBlockCallWasBypass, and AudioUnitSetProperty(BypassEffect) is NEVER issued by the lease. Grep
     confirms zero setBypassed/processBlockBypassed/isBypassed in ChainHost.cpp or LinkProcessor.cpp.
   Also: with ALL slots bypassed (lease engaged) hasActiveSlots_=false and ChainHost::process passes through BEFORE
     graph_->perform - the graph is not rendered at all during the full lease. (JUCE would otherwise render even
     disconnected nodes - graph :1129-1135 orders ALL nodes, skipping only isSuspended - but the early return prevents it.)
   NET, from the log + code: in the window switch -> crash the Link makes ZERO writes to the AU after its one prepare - no
     param, no bypass property, no post-prepare state. The AU is instantiated fresh, seeded while detached, prepared once,
     wired, rendered with real audio, and faults ~12-15s in, inside its own render, at fixed offsets. A write-during-render
     of ANY kind by us is now excluded. What remains is the RENDER INVOCATION / PREPARE CONFIGURATION itself (stock JUCE,
     nested on the AAE thread) - not any host write. No fix written; no theory promoted.

## 🔬 THE DIFF: one AU, every call, BUILD (completeLoad) vs SWITCH (planStageOne->tryReattachParked). Bounds the search.
   COMMON: construction is IDENTICAL on both — for a v2 AU createAudioUnit() ALWAYS uses AudioComponentInstanceNew on the
     message thread (the AudioComponentInstantiate async/out-of-process path is AUv3-ONLY, :484-500); async merely posts a
     message first. initialise(rate,bs) at construction = setRateAndBufferSizeDetails, setLatencySamples(0),
     refreshParameterList, setPluginCallbacks (HostCallbacks), createEventListener — NO AudioUnitInitialize (the init/uninit
     probe at :1076-1104 is canApplyBusesLayout, not reached). Q2 => sync-vs-async is NOT a difference. Eliminated.
   BUILD: createPluginInstanceAsync -> [msg thread] construct+initialise -> completeLoad{GraphMutation; addNode;
     slot.bypassed=attachBypassed_ (arrives in the lease's target state); rebuildGraph; graph prepareToPlay -> applySettings
     prepares it ONCE: prepareToPlay(sr,bs) = releaseResources(no-op) + setPluginCallbacks + SampleRate props +
     SetRenderCallback + MaxFramesPerSlice + resetBuses + syncBusLayouts + AudioUnitInitialize + ...} -> (later)
     setStateInformation/dial AFTER the one prepare -> first processBlock. INIT x1, UNINIT x0, PREPARE x1, never suspended.
   SWITCH: planStageOne{ sync createPluginInstance (same API/thread); proc->setPlayConfigDetails(2,2,sr,bs) [extra];
     suspendProcessing(TRUE); graph_->addNode — WITH NO GraphMutation (the only unguarded graph mutation found) } -> node sits
     in the graph SUSPENDED, UNSEEDED, NOT in preparedNodes -> Phase B REMOVE ops (parkSlotReattachable) run first and each
     calls graph prepareToPlay -> applySettings prepares EVERY node not in preparedNodes and does NOT check isSuspended
     (graph :461-483; isSuspended is render-time only, :887) => **INIT #1 ON A SUSPENDED, PARKED, UNSEEDED, UNWIRED NODE, in
     DEFAULT state** -> tryReattachParked{GraphMutation; suspendProcessing(false); setPlayConfigDetails [extra #2];
     setStateInformation(seed) onto an ALREADY-INITIALISED AU; direct prepareToPlay = releaseResources -> AudioUnitUninitialize
     [UNINIT #1] -> re-install callbacks/props -> AudioUnitInitialize [INIT #2]; **slot.bypassed = false** (ignores
     attachBypassed_ — arrives LIVE under the lease, unlike completeLoad); rebuildGraph wires it ACTIVE; graph prepare
     SKIPPED (now in preparedNodes)} -> lock released with the slot ACTIVE -> setSlotBypassed re-bypasses (own lock) ->
     lease remap/release -> first processBlock. INIT x2 (x3 if no Remove preceded), UNINIT x1, PREPARE x2-3, suspended
     true->false, 2 extra setPlayConfigDetails, callbacks/MaxFrames/SampleRate re-installed per prepare.
   ANSWERS: Q1 prepared twice = YES, init->uninit->init within ms (headless prepareToPlay always releaseResources() first, so
     no double-init on an initialised unit — but a full cycle). Q2 sync/async = no difference. Q3 = YES EXACTLY: first
     initialise while SUSPENDED+PARKED+UNSEEDED (default config), second after the seed — the AU's two initialises see
     DIFFERENT configurations; build sees one. Q4 extras: unguarded addNode in planStageOne; slot arrives LIVE (bypassed=false)
     and is wired active between two locks; 2 extra setPlayConfigDetails; seed lands between the two inits.
   No fix written. The build path proves we CAN host this AU; every candidate is in the list above.

## 🛠 BUILD-PATH-IS-THE-SPEC FIX (16 Sep 2026) — RED captured, code in, rebuilding:
   RED (extended attach_order vs the PRESERVED pre-fix lib = the no-reuse experiment build), 5 assertions fail:
     SWITCH (plan Create) mock timeline: "Construct PREPARE-WHILE-SUSPENDED SetState Prepare" — Prepare==2, ONE OF THEM ON A
     SUSPENDED/PARKED NODE (Q3 verbatim: addNode into the prepared graph auto-prepares the staged node at planStageOne, before
     the seed), seed BETWEEN the two prepares. Reuse re-add also Prepare==2 (direct + graph). BUILD path: exactly one
     prepare, never suspended, zero releases, seed after — the spec, unchanged.
   CONSTRAINT FOUND: JUCE's AudioProcessorGraph::Node::processor is PRIVATE (compile error) — a parked node cannot be
     lifted out of the graph and re-added. So the entry is DUAL-FORM: FRESH-staged (plan Create = the crash path) holds a
     bare unique_ptr OUTSIDE the graph -> at attach: seed -> addNode -> bypassed=attachBypassed_ -> rebuild -> the graph
     prepares it ONCE (pure completeLoad sequence). PARKED-removed (reuse, ELIMINATED as the cause) stays in-graph
     suspended + released (preparedNodes makes applySettings skip it = inert) -> at attach: seed detached -> ONE direct
     prepare -> bypassed=attachBypassed_ -> wire. Per the ruling "if the borrow pool still parks in-graph, that path keeps it".
   REMOVED: the direct prepareToPlay in tryReattachParked's fresh path; both explicit setPlayConfigDetails (completeLoad has
     none); planStageOne's addNode + suspend; tryReattachParked/borrowTryReuseInto `slot.bypassed = false` (now
     attachBypassed_, no lock-free live window); applyRestoredState's reused re-prepare (a reused node now gets one prepare
     at reattach and the restore seed lands after it — the build path's own proven order).
   GUARD: tools/attach_order extended — mock records Prepare vs PREPARE-WHILE-SUSPENDED (isSuspended() sampled at prepare),
     Release, SetState, Process; asserts per switch-path instance Prepare==1, Release==0, never-prepared-suspended, seed <
     the single prepare, never rendered before/under the lease, bypassed==attachBypassed_; and that the BUILD sequence is
     unchanged. (addNode/suspendProcessing are non-virtual — covered by the observable equivalents, noted in the file.)
   GREEN (same guard, NEW lib 20:58): SWITCH "Construct SetState Prepare" — Prepare==1, Release==0, never suspended, seed
     before the single prepare, arrives bypassed==attachBypassed_, not rendered under the lease, renders on release, still
     one prepare across the whole lease cycle. REUSE re-add "SetState Prepare SetState" — one prepare after the reset.
     BUILD unchanged "Construct Prepare SetState". 0 failures. RED->GREEN on one harness against the two libs.
   REGRESSION (one binary 20:58, one run, all GREEN): own-channel byte-diff = 18f25706a5886da54919b8d52aebd8e462c4c07a6f424e40ad1476dfe4e660c9
     EXACT; four-state latch GREEN; Part-A EQ readback GREEN; pre-gain send-half GREEN; attach_order GREEN. Nothing else moved.
   BUNDLES BUILT + STAGED (spec-fix, unsigned): V2 x86_64 4D35C922-D526-31B7-BE24-6B87972C8B59 / arm64 AFE31B07-8982-3364-9DC5-682E41EC9DB6;

     (V2 46M, Link 29M) + INSTALL_AAX_2026-09-16_specfix.command (verifies BOTH). PROVEN 3 WAYS: OK rc0 / MISMATCH rc1 (vs installed
     no-reuse 505A3C54, STOP) / NOT FOUND rc2. Desktop: EchoJay-{V2,Link}-AAX-unsigned-2026-09-16-specfix.zip + installer, ditto -c -k,

     5. spec sequence: EJNoReuse=0 (real reuse path), 7 Link "(plan attach)" lines (tryReattachParked ran), 6 stage lookups,
        no lease-invariant violations. Real AUs emit no per-prepare log line; the sequence is proven by the loaded binary being
        the one attach_order took RED->GREEN (Prepare==1, never suspended) — stated as such, not overclaimed.
     6. crash reports since install: ONE, 21:22:20 — NOT this build and NOT our signature: images = OLD no-reuse build
        505a3c54/78dd6a0b (a Pro Tools process alive during the 21:21:07 install), ProTools_Red>Foundation>libxpc>libdispatch
        on an unnamed dispatch thread, PAC-failure wild address, ZERO EchoJay frames, no plugin offsets — the known PT XPC
        host class (three on 09-10), consistent with bundles replaced under a running PT. No crash in the 21:26 session.

   MECHANISM (the diff, build path = spec): the switch path addNode'd the staged instance into the prepared graph at
     planStageOne; JUCE applySettings ignores isSuspended, so it INITIALISED the AU while SUSPENDED, PARKED, UNSEEDED, in

     under GraphMutation -> bypassed=attachBypassed_ -> one graph prepare) removed the crash. Repeated switches, Mike-E Comp

## ⚖ RULING ON THE FOUR COMMITS (17 Sep 2026, late) — answers A–E, amendments 2b/3b, COMMIT 4 proof.
A. THE AUDIBLE OBJECT WHILE HELD is V2's BorrowHost, by the call path: the Link's processBlock still calls
chainHost.process (LinkProcessor.cpp:1712 mono / :1742 stereo) but rackLeaseEngage (LinkProcessor.cpp:2004-2017)
setAttachBypassed(true) + setLeaseBypass(i,true) on EVERY slot — the Link streams DRY ("RACK engaged … streaming
dry"); V2 consumes that dry ring (PluginProcessor.cpp:1091 ringConsume) and runs borrowHost_->process(view, noMidi)
(PluginProcessor.cpp:1103) then injects it into the main buffer (:1299-1330) ahead of its own chainHost.process
(:1349). The Link's ChainHost is not bypassed as an object; each slot is lease-bypassed (route-around), so it
passes dry. SYMPTOM 2B (audible live while editing): CLOSED — not by Step 0's finding (no V2→Link writes exist
during a borrowed edit) but because the edit lands in the BorrowHost, which IS the object being heard; the Link
need not hear it until release. After COMMIT 3, onSlotWet's borrowed branch (PluginEditor.cpp:2102-2113) performs
BOTH writes: bh->setSlotWet(i, v, User) on the BorrowHost AND sendLinkSlotWetCommand(uid, i, slotIdentityHex(i), v)

## 🔒 COMMIT 2c — THE GATE OPENS ON THE ENGAGED FACT; THE SELECTION'S WANT PERSISTS (17 Sep 2026, V2 only).
SPEC CORRECTION (ruling): "orange" == the borrow session is engaged for the selected uid (PluginEditor.cpp:7812
paints the pill from v.borrowed), NOT rackLockState()==Held.
STEP 0 (tools/overlay_step0, real objects, seeded Link uid TLINKOV with a borrowCapable sidecar):
  [after select (sync)] lock=Held heldFor=1 want=TLINKOV pending=TLINKOV borrowActive=0 gateRefuses=0 editBlocked=0 overlay=0 pillOrange=0
  -> the lock is taken SYNCHRONOUSLY inside the select (setRackLockWant attempts NOW); the Held-but-not-engaged
     window opens at once with the COMMIT 2 gate already OPEN: blue rack, no overlay (symptom 1).
  [right after X (onRemoveSlot(0) in the window)] identical state; want/pending intact; the X took the remote fork
     (sendRackEdit: lock held -> chain-cmd remove to the Link, pollLinkBlockAck) - no setRackLockWant({}), nothing
     cleared. 1-slot rack: the borrow read stays in flight (no Link answers the pull in the harness). 0-slot rack:
     engaged at the first tick (EJBorrow: engaged uid=TLINKOV).
  LIVENESS (overlay_liveness_guard leg b2, RED): after an AUTO-RELEASE with the rack still selected
     (borrowRelease(false), the lease-loss shape): lock=Idle want= pending= borrowActive=0 editBlocked=1
     overlay=1 pillOrange=0, and 12 ticks never re-engage -> "overlay up, never orange" = symptom 2.
     Mechanism: pending is cleared at engage ("cleared ONLY once the session is live"), so borrowRelease
     (PluginProcessor.cpp:2424) clears the want; nothing re-arms from the selection.
FIX: ChainEditGate.h chainEditableFor(isLinkRack, state, borrowed): own always; a Link rack ONLY when borrowed;
chainLockStateText(state, borrowed): Idle/Held-not-engaged -> "Connecting to rack…", WaitRecency -> "Waiting for
audio…", HeldByOther -> "Rack held by another EchoJay", engaged -> "". Callers pass the pill's fact: the panel
(v.borrowed, :7636), chainEditGateRefusesFor (borrowHostIfActiveFor(uid) != nullptr, every callback guard), the
Build router and applyChainEditToLink refusals. LIVENESS: borrowSelectionTick re-arms pendingAutoEngage_ +
setRackLockWant(view) when a Link rack is selected, not engaged, lock Idle and no want (PluginProcessor.h gains
rackLockWantUid()). borrowRelease's own clear is unchanged (correct for deselect).
GUARDS: overlay_gate_harness (borrowed axis): RED on the COMMIT 2 header (no matching function), GREEN after;
overlay_liveness_guard (real objects): RED "(d) window: editBlocked == !pillOrange [editBlocked=0 pillOrange=0]"
and "(b2) ... RE-ENGAGES [tick -1]"; GREEN after (see commit).

## 🔧 HURDLE 1 ROUND (17 Sep 2026, 16:00-17:30): dial-only means everything dials — V2 items 1-4 + server D6/D7/near + D8. NOTHING pushed, NOTHING deployed.
Branch merge/kathy-2026-09-06 (the working branch since 6 Sep). Plugin commits 5484dae (item 1), c2aebd6 (item 2), a5ac83e
(item 3), 327bb3f (item 4); server branch server/2026-09-17-reverb-rotation commits d955a97 (D6), e548f76 (D7), 2a176cc
(near maps), 1170329 (D1 correction), ae34496 (slot-wet pins).
ITEM 1 (apply waits for maps). Cited path: ChainHost::setSlotStructuredSettings applied BEFORE kicking any fetch
(transient "fetch_requested=n -> noMap"), the exact fetch and the fallback lookup shared pendingMapFps_, and the SESSION
build path (PluginEditor.cpp:28536) logged its summary synchronously: Pure Plate 15:06:12.347 attach / .425 summary
"applied=0 status=pending" / .866 map stored / 3 of 4 applied by the 6 s watchdog. Fix: fetch first (exact + fallback in
parallel, own ledgers, 4 s bound each = kMapFetchBoundMs), apply second; whenDialSettled(4000) for the session verdict;
noMap only after the fetches answer or the bound. GUARD dial_wait_guard RED on the pre-round tree (6 fails, incl. the
shared-ledger defect: a lookup MISS settled the slot noMap while the exact fetch was still out) -> GREEN.
ITEM 2 (near-map acceptance). Cited path: EJFallback "no exact map" -> POST /api/params/lookup, product tiers by
format|uid -> "tier miss, reason fewer_params" for AudioUnit|76720177|10.5.0 (15:06:07.014); the two same-name registry
entries never tried. Fix: the entry carries the name; the server's miss row lists same-name maps ("near", each with
essential_plumbing); the client accepts the candidate whose names ALL resolve on the live instance (exact, case-
insensitive), re-indexed by name, tagged anchors_unverified (the apply path re-checks the live name at every index);
choice = most names resolving, tie -> no essential control classed plumbing/hidden, then offered order; logged
"EJDial: NEAR MAP accepted fp=.. from=.. params=n/n chosen by: .." / "REJECTED .. unresolved=[..]". GUARD near_map_guard
RED (5) -> GREEN (12 ok): (a) accepted + probe moved to 0.200, (b) rejected on one unresolvable name, (c) 5/5 beats
4/5, (d) tie -> the clean candidate, (e) item 3 below.
ITEM 3 (NOT DIALABLE). ChainHost::dialSummaryRow is the one row composer; under dial-only, a third-party slot whose
fetches answered with no map and no accepted near map reads "NOT DIALABLE (no map for fp, no near map)" (or ", near
map rejected: unresolved [..]"); SlotDialInfo.notDialable; the reply card gives it its own sentence naming the built-in
by role (NotDialableText.h) and paints coral (ChatBubbleStyle.h, ChatMsg.dialWarning). Never said while a fetch is out.
ITEM 4 (budget). 16384 samples = 341.3 ms @48k, reported as added latency while ACTIVE (one PDC re-run); the ring
alignment target 1024 is independent (pad = budget - ringAge - chainLat). Fix: capableLinkSeen_ persisted in the plugin
state, set when the capable listing first arrives; restored -> WANTED before prepareToPlay -> the budget commits at
prepare, no stop. The first session keeps the stopped-block path (ruled). GUARD borrow_budget_guard leg 5 RED
("OFF - nothing persisted") -> GREEN; legs 1-4, 6 GREEN; +16384 latency read back.

## 🔎 AUTO-TUNE PRO DID NOT DIAL on 17d + ae34496 (17 Sep 2026, 17:52-17:53 turn) - observation, then the RULING round
OBSERVED (unified log, EchoJay V2, verbatim order): 17:53:12.230 EJFallback: asking for slot 1 ("Auto-Tune Pro") fp
39b94e88ec79 -- no exact map | .231 EJDial: slot 0 ("Auto-Tune Pro") NO MAP for fp=39b94e88ec79 fetch_requested=y
in_flight=y -> pending (x2) | 17:53:17.762 ... in_flight=n -> noMap | .899 EJFallback: no map for
AudioUnit|76720177|10.5.0 -- tier miss, reason fewer_params | .899 EJDial: NEAR MAP REJECTED fp=39b94e88ec79
from=3018b13eaf2b ("Auto-Tune Pro") params=51/114 unresolved=[Master Bypass, HP Voice 1 Fixed Interval, ... HP
Bypass Harmony Player, HP Mute Input, HP Solo Input, HP Naturalize, HP Pitch Variation, HP Timing Variation, HP
Transition Time, Modern Scale, HP Attack, HP Release, HP Equalizer Type, HP LPF Frequen<...> (the log store truncates
the message at 1018 chars) | 17:53:22.152 EJDialSummary: SESSION build (borrowed host) complete, dial settled, 7
slot(s) [dial-only] / slot 0 ("Auto-Tune Pro") keys=[params{correction_mode, key_root, scale, reference_hz,
reference_source, ref_manual_by_user}, controls{Key, Retune Speed}] requested=8 applied=0 status=noMap localMap=n
nearMap=near map rejected: unresolved [...] | no EJParamApply line for slot 0. The slot ENDED noMap (near map
rejected 51/114); item 3's NOT DIALABLE predicate did not fire because the row's nearMapNote reads "near map
rejected..." - that case was "NOT DIALABLE (no map for fp, near map rejected: ...)" and it was appended (row shows
nearMap=...) but the CARD never went red: the borrowed-host build path composes no result bubble through
finishChainBubbleWhenDialSettled (that composer is the OWN-rack path; the SESSION path logs the summary only), so
the model's prose card stood alone. LOOKUP EXCHANGE: the client sent {ik AudioUnit|76720177|10.5.0, fp 39b94e88...,
name "Auto-Tune Pro", manufacturer, param_count, param_names[live]}; the miss row carried ONE same-name candidate
(3018b13e..., 114 names, essential_plumbing []) - the other registry entry (13aec649...) was not offered (no map
body under plugin:<fp>:map, or a different sameNameKey; the server's [params-lookup] nearOffered line was not
retrievable). Resolution: 51 of the candidate's 114 names resolve on the live instance; the 63 unresolved (48
visible) are Harmony Player controls (HP ...), Master Bypass and Modern Scale - the mapped build is a newer
Auto-Tune Pro with the Harmony Player, the live 10.5.0 lacks them (the product tier said the same: fewer_params).
Key and Retune Speed are NOT in the unresolved list (they resolved). Live instance names could not be quoted from a
probe: Auto-Tune Pro refuses to instantiate outside a host ("An OS error occurred during initialisation (-1)"); the
client sent them in the lookup body but does not log them. PRODUCTION LOG for the turn: not retrievable - `vercel
logs` shows ~100 recent rows (community-poll every 20 s floods it), the deployment events API returned [] for the
window and the runtime-logs endpoint HTTP 000; the client dump (chat-body-debug.json 17:58, autoDial true) shows
mapFps carried Auto-Tune Pro -> 39b94e88 and the server (ae34496 D7) admitted it BY SAME NAME. CAUSE: dial-only
was decided by the same-name index while the only same-name map cannot dial this binary; the client found that out
at apply time, after the model had chosen. (Proposed fix = the ruling below.)
RULING ROUND (no build, no deploy): SERVER fc4f5ea D7 STRICT (fp mapped or CLIENT-VERIFIED near map; same-name-only
excluded + substituted; body.verifiedNear parsed like mapFps; test-auto-dial-fp RED 4 -> GREEN 16 on the real
assemblePrompt; test-auto-dial's 11-Aug revert pins superseded). PLUGIN e38b1f0 (dial info kept per uid - the
"No suggested settings" bug), a624ac6 (item 1 pre-chat verification), ce0afde (item 3 + amendment). DEVIATION
FLAGGED: the verifier instantiates on the MESSAGE THREAD, one plugin at a time, 250 ms apart - JUCE AU/VST3
instantiation cannot run on a worker; the scan index holds no parameter names so no index-only path exists.
SUITE on the round lib (18:38): dialinfo_keep_guard, substitute_guard, near_verify_guard, near_map_guard (e updated
to the amendment), dial_wait_guard, borrow_budget_guard, borrowkept_harness, ui_guard, overlay_liveness_guard,

PRO TOOLS HANG (17 Sep 20:50:44, SESSION build on Link b32932c05f, slot 3 "AVOX SYBIL"): sample
~/Desktop/pt_hang_sybil.txt (932/932 samples). MAIN THREAD: juce::MessageQueue::runLoopSourceCallback ->
juce::AudioPluginFormat::handleMessage -> juce::AudioUnitPluginFormat::createPluginInstance -> AudioComponentInstanceNew
-> Synchronously -> instantiate -> APComponent::newInstance -> APComponent_FromBundle_Loadable::ResolveFactoryFunction
-> _CFBundleLoadExecutableAndReturnError -> _CFBundleDlfcnLoadBundle -> dyld4::APIs::dlopen_from ->
runInitializersBottomUpPlusUpwardLinks -> ... findAndRunAllInitializers -> ??? (in AVOX SYBIL) +0x2c05a6 -> +0x2c0c96
-> ??? (in Rosetta JIT). It sits INSIDE AVOX SYBIL 4.2.0's own static initialisers (com.Antares.Sybil, an x86_64
binary under Rosetta), reached from JUCE's AU instantiation on the message thread - not a dialog, not iLok frames, not
ChainHost, not a map-fetch wait, not the borrow/lease path. Because dlopen holds dyld's loader lock, Pro Tools'
Thread_18558633 (DigiPlatformSupport InitSymbolicator -> CoreSymbolication -> _dyld_process_info_create ->
dlopen_from -> _os_unfair_lock_lock_slow) is blocked behind it. Last EchoJay lines: 20:50:44.810 "EJChain: build
target uid=b32932c05f path=SESSION (borrowed host) removes=0 adds=7" / .828 EJPlace Auto-Tune Pro (REUSED from the
pool) / .842 EJPlace EchoJay EQ (EXACT built-in apply, 3 bands) / .844 "EJPanel: rebuild slots=2 sel=-1" - then
silence: SYBIL's createPluginInstance never returned. Whole app: the main thread (UI, transport control) is hung; the
audio engine is alive (AAE CCoreAudioDevice::CoreAudioIOProc samples present, HAL semaphore waits normal).
PROPOSAL (a) - it is SYBIL's own init: an out-of-process pre-flight - before a build instantiates a third-party
plugin not on the known-good list, tools/au_instantiate_probe runs it in a helper process with a 10 s timeout; a
hang/failure marks the plugin slow/bad in the disabled-set note and the build substitutes (item 3's path). Not fixed.

## 19 Sep 2026 — four findings from Sean's live mix-bus build (10:31:23Z on ok5vqa41d); branch server/2026-09-19-live-findings off main ce08d47 (7f944ec, 664e250; NOT pushed, NOT deployed)
- **A (observe, AMEK dial)**: pass 1 wrote controls.Ratio/Threshold/Attack (dropped: "not a mapped control"); pass 2 wrote controls.Ratio 1 / Threshold 1 / Timing 1 from the exposed map: Threshold 1 kind anchored range [0, 20] unit db (anchors 0→0 … 20→1); Timing 1 range [1, 7] unit "ms" (a 7-position timing selector labelled ms); Ratio 1 range [1.1, 40] unit null. Client: `EJParamApply: slot 1 ("AMEK Mastering Compressor"), 3 result(s)` / `EJRangeCheck: … requested=3 outOfRange=0` / `EJDialSummary: slot 1 … keys=[controls{Ratio 1, Threshold 1, Timing 1}] requested=3 applied=3`. "−15 dB" became 15 and "30 ms" became 7 at pass 2: the model re-expressed the prose within the exposed ranges (0..20 db, 1..7 ms); nothing clamps or converts. Proposal: dial compressors from the GR target + measured input level with a known threshold scale (as BuiltinIntentTranslate does), applied to third-party maps whose threshold unit/scale is known; Timing-style selectors need a `steps`/labels entry, not a ms range.
- **B (observe, loop not armed)**: no `EJLoudness: armed` line in the unified log at 11:32 BST; the limiter slot's settings text is REPLACED at built-in apply (ChainHost.cpp:3948 `s.settings = "Applied automatically\n" + summary`, summary = "input_db 8.8, ceiling_db -0.1, …" from EedDeviceProcessor applyParams) before armLoudnessLoopIfTargeted runs at build finish, and LoudnessLoop::armFromChain reads getSlotInfo(i).settings → targetFromSettingsText finds no "lufs target" → returns false, no bubble. The server string received was "input gain +8.8 dB to the -9 LUFS target (Commercial), ceiling -0.1 dBTP, true peak on; EchoJay Limiter last so the level loop can set the level". Fix (18d): arm from the structured params (params.target_lufs emitted by the server beside input_db) or keep the server text in a separate field the apply path does not overwrite; guard: arm after an EXACT built-in apply.
- **C (fixed, 7f944ec)**: live line genreSource "fallback default (-9)" while the classifier read hip_hop: the plugin sends no body.genre on chat-stream and no [LIVE METER: X (Genre)] header on a build; the genre is in the system-role message ("Internal genre reference (NEVER say this in your response): hip-hop."). The pass now reads parseGenre over the newest user text, then the system messages, then the classification's genre (both endpoints pass it). Guard RED on ce08d47 (2 legs, "to the -9 LUFS target") → GREEN (126 ok).
- **D (fixed, 664e250)**: Mix Bus / Master Bus cards drop the reference axis: intent, amount, loudness (three questions, loudness last). Reference-aim / specificity / typed-reference legs move to Instrument Bus. Guard RED on ce08d47 (8 legs) → GREEN (461 passed).
- **18d proposal (not implemented)**: per-slot input trim in ChainHost (a gain stage before every slot, dialled like a param); the loop drives the LAST slot's trim, not EchoJay Limiter's input_db; any limiter allowed last when its map exposes a ceiling control (the server sets ceiling there), else substituted by EchoJay Limiter; a true-peak check on the chain output with a "limiter not holding — swap?" bubble. Files: ChainHost.h/.cpp (slot trim + getSlotInfo/setSlotTrim + arm data), LoudnessLoop.h (drive trim; arm from structured target), PluginEditor.cpp (bubble + swap verb), EedLimiterProcessor untouched; guards: loudness_loop_harness (trim path, arm-after-apply, TP-not-holding bubble), overlay/slotwet precedent for the ctrl verb. Server relaxes once 18d is installed: the pass keeps a third-party limiter last when its map carries a ceiling control (sets Ceiling + emits target_lufs), and stops replacing.

- **G (no map edit)**: the AMEK "Timing 1" entry is NOT a 7-position selector: 21 tapered anchors 1.0, 1.1, 1.2 … 6.2, 7.0 (a continuous timing knob whose getText reads in "ms"); the model's "30 ms" became 7 by re-expression at the top of the 1..7 range. Scan of the 59 cached maps for anchored + integer-ended range (span 2–12, non-negative) + time/level unit: 9 hits — AMEK Timing 1 / Timing 2 (1..7 ms, continuous), CLA-76 (m) Analog (50..60 hz, 2 anchors = a 50/60 switch), L2 (s) Quantize (16..24 "ms", 5 anchors = 24/22/20/18/16 bit), Pro-Q 3 Analyzer Tilt (0..6 db, 5 anchors), UAD Ampex ATR-102 Cal Level (3..9 db, 4 anchors), UAD Studer A800 Cal Level (3..9 db, 4 anchors), UAD Neve 2254 E Lim Lvl (4..12 db, 17 anchors, 0.5 steps), UAD Neve 33609 C Lim 1 Thr (4..15 db, 23 anchors, 0.5 steps). The map format has NO stored selector field for anchored controls: steps are DERIVED at serve time by params-lib enumeratedSteps (anchors on an even normalised grid, M ≤ 19 positions, strictly increasing values) — it yields [3, 6, 7.5, 9] for the two Cal Levels and null for CLA-76 Analog (2 anchors), L2 Quantize (values descend) and Analyzer Tilt (grid fails); `kind: "mode"` with `labels` exists only for switches the extractor classed as modes. Proposal: a stored `positions: [{value, label}]` on anchored controls with ≤ 12 distinct getText readings, recorded at extraction from the getText strings (the label as the plugin prints it, e.g. "50 Hz"/"60 Hz", "24 bit"), rendered by fmtControl as the brace form and applied by position; the mapping UI shows the readings for such a control and asks Sean to confirm "discrete" or "continuous" — no hand-editing of ranges or units.

## 19 Sep 2026 — 18f FIX (client UI only, merge/kathy-2026-09-06; ChainHost untouched → V2 only)
- **Item 3** (EedLevelProcessor.h, EedLevelEditor.h/.cpp): the Level card's second row is "OUT → limiter" and a third row shows the limiter's current GR ("limiter GR X dB", "–" until the loop ticks); the device carries `setDownstreamGrDb` (atomic, NaN default), fed by the loop's tick from the EchoJay Limiter's gainReductionDb.
- **Items 1, 2, 4 + the quiet-window and back-off asks** (LoudnessLoop.h, PluginEditor.h/.cpp): `Bubble` gained `kind` (progress / arm / proposal / result / stuck / quiet / backoff / info) and `pills`; only progress bubbles replace (and only the previous progress bubble); every other bubble is history, appended with its pills, and an open progress bubble is closed first. Pill sets: proposal [Go][Leave it], result ALWAYS [Undo][A bit louder][A bit softer][Push it] (on target included), stuck [Push it][Leave it], quiet [Listen again][This is the loudest part], back-off [Back off][Leave it]. A pill tap calls handleLoudnessVerb(label) — the same handler as the typed word — and the composer is cleared before the local user bubble. The quiet window (input ≥ 3 dB under the build-time input) now ASKS (state quietAsked, measurement held) instead of silently restarting: "This is quieter than the section the chain was built on (−X now vs −Y at build). Is this the loudest part of the song?"; typed equivalents "listen again" / "this is the loudest part" / "back off". Tracking over-target proposes "That section is louder than the one I levelled on (… over the target) - back off −Z dB?". The verb line ("verb \"go\" state 3") now rides the loop's own EJLoudness stream (`LoudnessLoop::note`) so a pill and a typed word are one comparable sequence (found by the ui_guard leg: the editor's NSLog was outside the hook).
- One commit for items 1/2/4 + the asks (flagged): they are one change to the bubble contract (the emit signature and every emit site changed once); item 3 is its own commit.

## 20 Sep 2026 — 18h OBSERVE
- **(a) the "large empty dark panel" is the [Go] pill.** Reproduced headless (a diagnostic harness built the targeted chain in the Chat tab, listened on seeded noise to a proposal, typed "push it", and snapshotted the editor): `layoutResultChips` gave a chip `w = min(remaining, textWidth + 24)` and, when that came under 40 px, `w = max(40, remaining)` - the rule that let the single "Suggest an alternative" chip take the whole bubble. "Go" measures ~14 px + 24 = 38 < 40, so it took the REMAINING BUBBLE WIDTH: in the Chat tab (chat-only mode, maxBubbleW ≈ 1,130 px) a 1,130 × 26 px dark rounded button with "Go" centred - Sean's panel - and "Leave it" laid out beyond the right edge (x = 1,248 in a 1,200 px area), clipped. The Chain side panel's bubble is ~400 px wide, so the same bar looked like a wide pill there and "Leave it" fell off its edge too (the 18g snapshot showed the five result pills clipped at the panel's right). It was the result-chip row of the loop bubble (the 18f pills ride the alternatives-chip pool), never a chain-card container; it held nothing because it IS one button.
- **(b) "limiter working ~0.0 dB (estimated)" on Newfangled Elevate.** The estimate was mean(Level OUT LUFS-S − chain OUT LUFS-S) per tick over the window, both from 3 s short-term rings updated per block on the same audio (aligned; the Level device's tally is reset with the window since 18h, before it held since arm). A limiter that gives the level back (Elevate's adaptive/level-matched output) or works only on the hits leaves the LOUDNESS difference at ~0 while its GR trace shows work - the hits are milliseconds inside a 3 s window. Fix (item 5): the PEAK difference beside it - Level OUT true peak minus chain OUT true peak, both max-held over the window - "limiter working ~X dB (estimated), up to ~Y dB on the hits". The fixture proved the principle in miniature: white noise through a −0.5 dBFS clipper still reads +5.7 dBTP (intersample overshoot), the loudness difference is 0.58 dB and the peak difference 1.11 dB, both matched by an independent meter on a bypass rig. A map-exposed GR readout (where a limiter publishes one) is proposed, not built.

## 20 Sep 2026 — OBSERVE: a deleted chain comes back (Nafe Lead Vocal Link). No fix.
- **Timeline (Pro Tools process, unified log).** Session 16:12–18:17 BST, reopen 18:56 (the ruling's "~19:00–19:30" is not in the log). 16:12:53 the three Nafe Links get their names (Lead Vocal uid ba5cdcab8b); 16:12:57 one Link rebuilds a 6-slot vocal chain from its chunk (`EJChatLink: build start` → EQ, Lindell 902, MC 77, TCL-2, AVOX WARM, FlexVerb). Pro Tools fetched EVERY Link's chunk three times: **16:14:44, 16:16:22, 16:17:22** (40 `getState` lines each) — and never again, though the session ran to 18:17. 18:56:05 the Links re-mint uids (Lead Vocal e28405a535); 18:56:09 the same 6-slot chain rebuilds from the chunk. There is NO remove / plan-op / borrow / lease / chain-cmd line for any rack in the whole session: none of the three delete paths writes an EJ line (Link editor `removeChainSlot`, chain-cmd `pollChainCommand`, BorrowHost `removeSlot`), and `EJLease: RACK engaged` / `EJBorrow: engaged` never appear — the borrow path did not run as far as the log can see. The chain_archive holds one entry from 25 Aug; no "chain replaced" today.
- **What the Link saved.** `getStateInformation` (LinkProcessor.cpp:2707) serialises `chainModelToVar()` = `chainModel` (editor-facing model kept in step with the host by `removeChainSlot` / `syncModelAfterStructuralChange`); `setStateInformation` (≈2844) rebuilds ONLY from the chunk's `chain` array (`restoreChainFromVar`). The log had no slot count on either → **added** `EJLinkState: getState uid=… chainModel=N slot(s), host=M, lease=…` and `setState … chain=N slot(s) in the chunk` (LinkProcessor.cpp, uncommitted-to-binary: needs the build ruling).
- **Sidecars.** ~/Library/EchoJay/chain_plugins.xml (405 B, one AUDelay entry) and chain_entries.xml (879 kB, mtime 6 Sep) are the KnownPluginList caches — plugin LISTS, not chain content. Per-uid files under ~/Library/Application Support/EchoJay/link/: rack-<uid>.json (the rack the Link PUBLISHES for the main plugin's view), audio_<uid>.bin (meters), lease/ctrl/chain-cmd/ack. None is read on setStateInformation; the session chunk is the only source on load. Load order: chunk → callAsync restoreChainFromVar → buildChainFromSpec; sidecars are outputs. Session state already wins; nothing on disk resurrects.
- **Headless repro (tools/link_state_guard, against the Link's shipping archive; tools/borrow_delete_push_guard, V2 lib).** A (Link editor delete): host 0 / model 0, 4 dirty-marks, chunk 0 slots, restore brings nothing back. B (chain-cmd remove ×3): host 0 / model 0, dirty-marked, acks ok, chunk 0. D (a save DURING a rack lease): the chunk carries the 3 slots. E: a 3-slot chunk applied to a Link rebuilds 3 slots. C (V2 BorrowHost, `borrow_delete_push_guard`): engage, 3 slots, delete all → **no ctrl command with a structPlan before deselect (RED)**; `borrowApplyAndRelease` is "ONE COMPUTATION, at the moment of deselect/close" (PluginProcessor.cpp:2584); with the harness's originals seeded outside the pull the plan computed empty and the release sent nothing ("DEFECT empty plan" branch).
- **Cause, as far as the evidence reaches.** The chain came back from the Link's OWN chunk, and the chunk Pro Tools saved is the one fetched at 16:17:22 at the latest — at that moment the Link's `chainModel` still held the 6 slots. Both Link-reaching delete paths empty the model synchronously and dirty-mark the host (proven headless); had either run before 16:17:22 the chunk would be empty, and had either run after it Pro Tools would have re-fetched at the next save (the counter moved) — no fetch happened. The only path that removes slots without touching the Link's model or its change counter is the borrowed-rack session, whose plan is computed and sent only at deselect/close — but its engage/lease lines are absent from this session's log, so the log does not place Sean's deletes on it. Two readings remain: the deletes were made on a borrowed rack and the session was saved (or Pro Tools quit) before deselect; or the save Sean made was 16:17:22 and the deletes came after it. The slot-count logs now on get/setState settle this on the next run.
- **Proposed fix (three parts, each with a guard; not built).** (1) Every structural edit on a borrowed rack (remove / move / add) is sent to the Link at once as a chain-cmd v:2 op (the transport the Link tab already uses), the Link applies it through `syncModelAfterStructuralChange` (model + dirty-mark), and the deselect plan carries only state commits — guard: borrow_delete_push_guard's RED leg goes GREEN, plus a Link-side leg on a pull-seeded session showing the chunk at 0 before release. (2) Session state stays the sole source on load — guard: a rack-<uid>.json with 3 slots beside a 0-slot chunk restores 0 (link_state_guard). (3) Under a lease, `getStateInformation` serialises the rack as the main plugin currently has it (after (1) that is the model itself) — guard: link_state_guard D flips to 0 slots after V2-side deletes. Plus the slot-count logs stay.

## OWED (top): rebase-on-ack + idempotent chain commands — spec for the record, NOT built; rides with the next plugin round (both bundles: LinkProcessor changes)
- PluginProcessor.cpp ~2612: the session rebases in the ACK callback, not on write success. A missing or "failed" ack leaves the op pending; the deselect/close plan re-sends pending structural ops before its state commits.
- LinkProcessor::pollChainCommand: each command carries an id; a command whose id the Link has already applied is acked "ok" again but not re-applied (idempotent), so a lost ack never applies a delete twice.
- Guards (RED on current code, GREEN after): L1 Link not polling during a borrowed delete (pause its timer), V2 deselects, Link resumes → chunk has 5 slots. L2 ack lost after apply (drop the ack file), V2 re-sends at deselect → chunk still 5 and the "EJLink: applied" line appears once for that id. L3 existing G1–G5 unchanged.

VTAPE Speed: panel "0"/"1"/"2" (3 discrete steps), no ips text exposed; map lands 1→0.5→"1". Nine selectors: Pro-Q 3 Analyzer Tilt panel is a step index "0..4" (map says 0–6 dB; AU idx 351 vs map 375); CLA-76 Analog panel has an "Off" at 1.0 the map lacks; L2 Quantize is bits, reversed, unit "ms" wrong; ATR-102/Studer Cal Level, Neve 2254 Lim Lvl, Neve 33609 Lim 1 Thr match their anchors. Proposal (10 lines) delivered: scan-time `positions:[{norm,label}]` for discrete controls, kind "stepped", labels exposed and landed by label.

VST3: **0 scanned** — the shipped probe hard-codes `pluginFormatName = "AudioUnit"` (au_instantiate_probe.cpp), so every VST3 request was refused "No compatible plug-in format exists for this plug-in" (48 maps) or the plugin has no VST3 entry (18). VST3 needs a probe change (format from the file argument) = the next ship; decision for the ruling.
- **PACE 14 through the signed probe.** 2 loaded and matched in full (Empirical Labs Mike-E Comp 12/12, spiff 39/39). 12 were KILLED by the kernel: all 12 crash reports (~/Library/Logs/DiagnosticReports/EchoJayProbe-2026-09-21-1008xx.ips) share one identity — `SIGKILL (Code Signature Invalid)`, termination CODESIGNING "Invalid Page", faulting inside `handleWrapEvent` of an image the PACE wrapper created through NSCreateObjectFileImageFromMemory, reached from dyld's initialisers of the plugin. The wrapper maps executable pages that carry no signature and the hardened runtime refuses them. Pro Tools.app carries `com.apple.security.cs.allow-unsigned-executable-memory` (and allow-dyld-environment-variables); EchoJayProbe.entitlements carries only disable-library-validation. **No PACE dialog in the unified log** (no "100001" / "Fatal error" line from any EchoJayProbe process; each died 0.5–2 s after start, before the wrapper reached its UI). I cannot see the desktop: if a dialog is showing, say which plugin. The 12 were not retried. The entitlement is a signing change = next ship; decision for the ruling.

## 21 Sep 2026 — ITEM 2 (PACE / pre-flight OBSERVATION, no fix): none of the 12 SIGKILLed PACE plugins is disabled
- Read only. `~/Library/EchoJay/plugin_disabled_reasons.json` (276 B, 18 Sep 16:09): two entries, `low_control_unknown` and `avox_sybil_antares` — none of the 12. `~/Library/Application Support/EchoJay/plugin_disabled.json` (45 B, 18 Sep 10:57): the same two uids; its `plugin_disabled_reasons.json` (244 B): `avox_sybil_antares` only. `~/Library/Application Support/EchoJay/known_good.json` (26 entries, 20 Sep 18:59) marks 5 of the 12 known-good: AVOX WARM (2026-09-18), SSL Native FlexVerb (09-18), Newfangled Punctuate (09-18), SSL Fusion Vintage Drive (09-19), Newfangled Elevate (09-20). The other 7 (Decapitator, Eiosis E2Deesser, Harrison De-Esser, Lustrous Plates, Newfangled Saturate, SSL DeEss, SSL Native Vocalstrip 2) are neither known-good nor disabled.
- **Why pre-flight survives where `--list-params` dies (same signed binary, same createPluginInstanceAsync, same arch):** (1) a known-good mark comes from an IN-HOST create succeeding, not from the probe (`ChainHost.cpp:2713` markKnownGood after the host's own create), and pre-flight skips known-good plugins (`:7630`) — so the probe never runs on those 5. (2) For the rest, a probe that exits non-zero without touching the marker is `PreflightState::error` with the note "probe exit N (not evidence: licence-bound plugins fail out of process) - in-host create proceeds" (`ChainHost.cpp:7676`); only two consecutive TIMEOUTS mark hangs-on-load (`:7700`). The SIGKILL is an exit, not a timeout, so pre-flight tolerates it and the host loads the plugin itself; the scan treats the same exit as a refusal. No retry or format difference is involved (the retry at `:7690` is for timeouts only; `/usr/bin/arch -x86_64` only when the host is x86_64, `:7590`).
- **Owed for the next probe ship (no build now):** `com.apple.security.cs.allow-unsigned-executable-memory` in EchoJayProbe.entitlements and the sign helper (every ship since 19e carries disable-library-validation only); VST3 from the file argument; the newline before row 0.

- **Observation (Sean's account; the last 30 chain builds 16–21 Sep from `ej:chain-settings:<day>` + `recent_plugins` in the KV, read only; 18 of the 30 are Mix Bus, the rest Lead Vocal 4, Kick 4, Master Bus 2, Bass/808 1, unknown 1; all via chat-stream).**

- (2) The soft [CHAIN VARIETY] note was present on **30/30** turns, always carrying its cap of **8 names** (the head of the 30-name recent list = the last two chains only); the model used one of its names anyway on **8 of 30** turns. (3) The pool is presented **fixed, alphabetical by plugin name** (ChainHost sorts `entries_` by name, case-insensitive; the server never reorders it). (4) `recent_plugins` is written on both chain routes — chat-stream after a chain block (block names minus built-ins) and chat.js after a block or from prose mentions; no other route; Sean's list is at its **30** cap. (5) The 17 Sep "reverb rotation" is the `[CHAIN VARIETY (HARD)]` path: it fires only when the user's words ask for "something different", removes last-chain plugins of the same category from the feed, and has **not fired since** (0 `[chain-variety-hard]` rows in 14 days of logs).
- **Fix (d87fb9f):** `api/_rotation.js` — per role with ≥3 candidates, the names used for that role in the user's last 3 chains on this channel type leave the pool before the model sees it; never a plugin named this turn, never an EchoJay built-in; a role that would drop below 3 loses only the most recent one, below 2 none; the remaining pool is shuffled deterministically (seed = turn id); post-reply an excluded plugin still returned is swapped for the first remaining same-role candidate and logged `[rotation] swapped a → b role r`. Store `recent_by_role:{email}` = `{"<channel>|<role>": [[newest chain's names], …]}`, cap 10 per role, written on both routes. Assembly hook on chain_generate feed turns from plugin clients; `[chain-rotation]` log line; any failure leaves the pool untouched. Decisions flagged: applied on build turns only (an edit targets named things); chat.js runs the swap inside its auto-dial block (V2 sends autoDial true); the swapped slot keeps the model's settings text, so its structured controls may not fit the substitute (the client declines unknown controls).

- **FLAG (found by the real leg, not this round's fix):** the WaveShell AU's value and text reads are one write behind until the message loop runs. It is a native component, so ChainHost's stale-read gate (`auComponentIsBridged`) is off for it and the immediate readback compares the pre-write state: the EXISTING anchored path reverts too (Ratio 8 → "write did not stick, value restored" with the in-host setting; APPLIED and landed "8" with the stale-read setting). The R legs run with the stale-read setting and verify the panel after the AU settles; the in-host reading is printed as a FLAG line. No "value restored" lines in the Pro Tools log over 3 days (the client may not log that string). **Ruling needed:** extend the stale-read gate to plugins whose readback is observed pre-write (WaveShell), or re-read after a settle.

## 21 Sep 2026 — SETTLED READ-BACK (ruling: the WaveShell stale-read is a product bug) — 0e6d2cf; ship_2026-09-21k PACKAGED (unsigned)
- **Observation (ruled):** the number of "did not stick" reverts in Sean's dial logs over 7 days cannot be established from this machine — the unified log store holds no Pro Tools EJDial / EJParamApply lines in 14 days (0 rows; the retained EJDial lines are today's harnesses) and the server has 0 `[dial-declines]` rows for Sean's account in 7 days. In-host cost: unmeasured.
- **Fix (0e6d2cf):** verification of a landed value happens after a settle for every plugin, native or not. An immediate mismatch on any path (position with texts, position without, mode, setread, gettext) keeps the write and marks the result pending with the same test the immediate read failed; ChainHost schedules the re-read on the next message-loop tick (callAsync), then every 30 ms until settled or 250 ms; only then a still-mismatching value is restored, and the "did not stick" line carries both reads (`immediate read "20", settled read "8"`). The ledger is re-recorded from the settled results. The bridged report-only setting is removed from the verification (the parameter stays, ignored); no special case remains.
- **Guards:** stepped_position_guard R1/R2 with the shipping setting + the new A1 (real WaveShell anchored path). RED on the tree before: R1 "asked position 3 (Off), plugin shows 60Hz, value restored"; A1 Ratio 8 "write did not stick (norm round-trip failed), value restored", panel "20". GREEN: R1 applied after settle, reads "Off" (immediate "60Hz"); R2 reads "60Hz"; A1 Ratio 8 norm 0.500, panel "8", not reverted; P1–P6 unchanged. Touched: eq_readback GREEN, pregain_readback GREEN (unchanged), land_by_name GREEN, pair_dial GREEN.

## 22 Sep 2026 — SERVER ROUND (items 1–4): item 2 SHIPPED dpl_GDR7sKKVXA9a3M66iR1DBUpZcXaR, item 1 HELD (gate cannot pass), observations 3 and 4

- **Item 1, positions (server/2026-09-21-positions f1b39da): HELD, not deployed.** The gate needs the controls note to show CLA-76 Analog as Off / 50Hz / 60Hz, and that text comes from the REGISTRY's map (index:mapped-controls → lookup → fmtControl). `POST /api/params/lookup` for CLA-76 (m) (identity AudioUnit 3d307263, exact_fp) returns Analog as `{"kind":"anchored","index":5,"range":[50,60],"unit":"hz","anchors":[[50,0],[60,0.5]]}` — no `positions`, no `kind:position`; the client map (~/Library/EchoJay/param_maps.json) carries the same anchored entry. The server half prints panel texts only when the map has `positions` (from the probe's --list-steps read through the client's map builder — the round (c) map step, still owed, "no map changes" ruled). Deploying would fail the gate by construction and roll back. Branch is 1 commit behind main (base ce0752f); rebase when the map step lands.
- **Item 2, loop complaint routing (server/2026-09-22-loop-complaints 73f3597, 7d7ab88, bf28d43 → main): SHIPPED.** (a) classify.js: after the apply bubble ("Applied -3.0 dB (Level now +4.6). How's it sounding?") a typed complaint about limiting or level → intent loop_verb with `verb: 'back_off'`; the apply bubble now counts as a loop bubble for the plain verbs too; an edit request stays a model edit. RED on main f2eaffe 4 FAIL → GREEN. **Client half queued (next build):** the forced loop_verb path must map a complaint to the softer step ×2 (today it prints the verb hint). (b) _pass-loudness-limiter.js: on a target chain the last limiter keeps only its ceiling dial — numeric threshold / release / input gain stripped, "[loudness-limiter-strip]" logged, a labelled mode stays; RED → GREEN. (c) _chain-pass2.js: a pass-2 dial whose map entry is 0..1 with no unit and no labels (TCL-2 "Level 1" 0.65, "Threshold 1") is dropped, "[bare-normalised-dial]" logged; a unitless ratio (range 1..10) stays; RED → GREEN 147 + control. Suites: test-chain-post-passes 178/0, test-chain-pass2 147 + control; full suite on bf28d43 117 ok, 1 non-zero (turn-charge-lift, main's).
  - **Deploy:** main f2eaffe..bf28d43, pushed, **dpl_GDR7sKKVXA9a3M66iR1DBUpZcXaR** (https://echojay-saas-lvwkgvfhl-studioseand-4399s-projects.vercel.app, 10:53:03 BST), Ready. **Rollback line (not run): `vercel promote https://echojay-saas-o16sk3z9h-studioseand-4399s-projects.vercel.app --scope studioseand-4399s-projects --yes`** (= dpl_7mKJKK8m, main f2eaffe).
  - **Gates: PASS.** Live classify (Sean's token, synthetic channel): "too squashed" and "it sounds over limited now" after the apply bubble → loop_verb verb=back_off (RED on the previous deployment: 400 / not a loop verb); "too squashed" after an ordinary reply → ambiguous (not a loop verb); "go" after the apply bubble → loop_verb. Glue build: Pro-Q 3, Vertigo VSC-2 (vca/glue), API-560, Kramer Tape, Level, Elevate — 0 unresolved.
- **Item 3, OBSERVATION (Pro-Q 3 edit turn, 09:21:48 BST, /api/chat-stream, reqTurnType chain_edit, classifier → chain_edit).** The [AVAILABLE PLUGINS] block that turn: **1,077 names, Pro-Q 3 PRESENT** (the DEBUG-VOLATILE-SAMPLE carries it; [auto-dial-set] feed 1077). **The shortlist did NOT run** (no [shortlist] / [chain-rotation] line on the edit turn — correct). Validation set: pass 1 (assembly) **809 allowed** — byName 809, fpKnown 46, fpMiss 1, **byProduct 1** (Pro-Q 3: its fingerprint is not in the registry, the PRODUCT identity is, so the assembly allowed it); pass 2 (chat-stream `checkAutoDial`, after the model) **808 allowed, fpRefused 1, byProduct 0** → [auto-dial-violation] names ["pro-q 3"] → [auto-dial-refused]. The two passes differ because `_auto-dial-check.js:49 buildAutoDialSet({ feedNames, mapFps, mappedNames, registryByFp })` is called WITHOUT `productIds` / `mappedProducts`, while the assembly (`_chat-assembly.js:3705/3837/4147`) passes both. Exact refusal text: `api/_auto-dial.js:177` (`REFUSAL_CASES.not_owned.reason` = "it is not in this project's plugin list, so EchoJay cannot add it"), rendered by `enforceAutoDial` (`_auto-dial-check.js:132`). Pass 2 also lost pass 2's own work: chain-pass2 had resolved Pro-Q 3 from the bulk registry and dialled 5 band controls before the refusal. **One-line fix (proposed, not applied):** in `_auto-dial-check.js` `checkAutoDial`, add `productIds: bodyProductIds(body), mappedProducts: await getMappedProductsSet(),` to the `buildAutoDialSet` call — parity with the assembly. Open question: why the case was `not_owned` (the feed had the name; `refusalFor` should have answered `no_map`/`unresolved`) — the wording may have come from the client's mirror; not verified.
- **Item 4, OBSERVATION (EJLoudness, this Mac, 10:10:57–10:11:33 BST).** armed: target -8.0 LUFS (level_params, commercial), Level slot 4 gain +7.6 dB, limiter slot 5 (Limitless — third-party), build-time input -17.8 LUFS. measured (10:11:29): max short-term **-5.0 LUFS** (input window -16.8), needed -3.0 dB, Level +7.6, trim -3.0, "limiter GR avg 0.0 max 0.0 dB". Bubble: "Measured -5.0 LUFS (loudest 3 s). Push -3.0 dB to reach -8.0? limiter working ~0.0 dB (estimated), up to ~8.1 dB on the hits." Go at 10:11:32: Level +7.6 → +4.6. **No measurement after Go** (no Check was tapped: the loop holds after an apply by design) — so there are no after-Go numbers, and **the Level OUT / chain OUT true-peak values are not logged at all** (only their differences are). Why avg ~0.0 while hits 8.1: with a third-party limiter the loop cannot read GR, so the bubble uses two ESTIMATES (LoudnessLoop.h:386-388): the "average" is grEstimateDb() = mean of (Level OUT − chain OUT) in LUFS-S over the window — short-term loudness barely changes through a peak limiter, so ~0.0; "on the hits" is grPeakEstimateDb() = Level OUT true peak − chain OUT true peak, both max-held — the transients the limiter actually clipped, 8.1 dB. The measured line's "avg 0.0 max 0.0" is the EchoJay Limiter's measured GR, which is 0.0 whenever the limiter is third-party — a dead number printed as if measured. Client fix queued with item 5: the estimated line is logged beside it and the true-peak values are printed.
- **Item 6, OBSERVATION (Manley Massive Passive MST):** through the installed probe (AU, `--list-params`, 50 params) the live names are `Ch1HiMidGain` at index 12, `Ch1HiMidBW` at 13, `Ch1HiGain` at 17 — **identical to the map's names and indices** (map: 26 controls). No "EJMap: unresolved" line for Manley exists in this Mac's unified log for the last 3 days (the only unresolved lines are a harness's Pro-Q 3 "Ghost Knob"). Nothing to fix by evidence; the lines Sean saw need their source (which host, which format — the VST3 instance would carry different names).

## 22 Sep 2026 — PRO-Q 3 PARITY SHIPPED (server/2026-09-22-autodial-parity 3256ede → main): live dpl_9AHazvESAXaaAdg73dvwBvmfLZN7, gate PASSED after two FALSE rollbacks caused by the gate script itself
- **Fix (3256ede):** `buildAutoDialSetForBody({ feedNames, body, mappedNames, registryByFp })` in _chat-assembly.js is the ONE set builder (mapFps + productIds from the body, the mapped-products index); the three assembly sites and `checkAutoDial` (_auto-dial-check.js) call it. Legs (test-auto-dial-fp §4): the 09:21 shape (product mapped, fingerprint not in the registry) — RED on main bf28d43 "FAIL the post-model check ALLOWS … (Twinny): no violation -- ["Twinny"]" then TypeError (builder absent) → GREEN 16/0; PARITY leg (identical sets for the same feed and body); the one-builder source pin. Touched suites GREEN (auto-dial-fp 16/0, auto-dial 219, auto-dial-enforce 49, arms 174, readback 105/0, do-not-dial 73, hygiene 69/0); full suite 117 ok, 1 non-zero (turn-charge-lift).
- **The not_owned wording, in one line from the code:** `api/_auto-dial.js:356` — `refusalFor(name)` answers `not_owned` when the op's lower-cased name is not a token of the parsed [AVAILABLE PLUGINS] block (`feedLower`); the client's list carries BOTH "Pro-Q 3" (AudioUnit) and "FabFilter Pro-Q 3" (VST3) and the block's exact token is not captured (FEED_CAPTURE off; the DEBUG sample is cut at 102 of 1,077 names), so the pass-1 `byProduct 1` and the pass-2 `not_owned` are jointly consistent only with the model naming "Pro-Q 3" while the feed token differed (or the feed lacking the AU name) — unresolved by evidence. Parity alone would not have saved an op whose name is not a feed token; a second, un-ruled suggestion: let the violation check resolve the op's name through the same feed-name resolver the set uses (suffix / product tiers) before calling it a violation.
- **Deploy:** main bf28d43..3256ede, pushed, **dpl_9AHazvESAXaaAdg73dvwBvmfLZN7** (https://echojay-saas-ixjsxqlvq-studioseand-4399s-projects.vercel.app, 11:57:28 BST). **Rollback line (not run as a real rollback): `vercel promote https://echojay-saas-lvwkgvfhl-studioseand-4399s-projects.vercel.app --scope studioseand-4399s-projects --yes`** (= dpl_GDR7sKKV, main bf28d43).
- **Gate history (honest):** the Glue gate script REFUSED twice with its own precondition "feed names without a fingerprint", and my automatic-rollback branch treated rc 2 as a failed build — production was promoted back to dpl_GDR7sKKV at 11:58:37 and again at 11:59:12, then forward again. Cause: Sean's morning rescan added 12 maps (66 → 78) including a SECOND map for the plugin name "Pro-Q 3" (the VST3 twin), which doubled the name in the script's feed so its name→fingerprint table came up one short. Script fixed (feed de-duplicated by name; `fpKey` fallback), re-promoted at 11:59:38, **Glue gate PASS at 11:59:45** (UAD Neve 33609 C vca/vintage/glue, NEOLD BIG AL, AMEK EQ 250, Level, Limiter; 0 unresolved). Production = dpl_9AHazvES. Lesson filed: a gate script's own refusal (rc 2) is not a failed gate; the rollback branch now must distinguish "refused to run" from "ran and failed". Note for item 3: the client now HAS a Pro-Q 3 VST3 map (78 maps), so the 09:21 fingerprint miss may no longer reproduce as such.
